> Let me write the complete code now. I'll need to be very careful about the physical model for badminton shuttlecock flight.
> 
> The shuttlecock flight model:
> - Forces: gravity (mg), air drag (bv², where b depends on orientation)
> - The drag force is F_drag = -b * v² * (v/|v|) = -b * |v| * v
> - This gives acceleration: a = g - (b/m) * |v| * v
> 
> For the shuttlecock, the drag is significant because:
> - Mass m ≈ 5g = 0.005 kg
> - The drag coefficient is very high compared to a ball
> - Terminal velocity is about 6.5-7 m/s
> 
> The equations of motion:
> - dv_x/dt = -(b/m) * sqrt(v_x² + v_y²) * v_x
> - dv_y/dt = -g - (b/m) * sqrt(v_x² + v_y²) * v_y
> 
> Where b is the drag coefficient (kg/m), and m is mass (kg).
> 
> For a typical shuttlecock:
> - m = 0.005 kg
> - b ≈ 0.001 kg/m (this gives terminal velocity ~7 m/s: mg = b*v_term² → v_term = sqrt(mg/b) = sqrt(0.005*9.81/0.001) ≈ 7 m/s)
> 
> Let me now write the comprehensive code. I'll structure it carefully.
> 
> Actually, let me think about what "真正共同拟合" means. The user wants all methods to genuinely work together, not just parallel+select. Some approaches:
> 
> 1. **Joint parameter optimization**: All methods contribute to a shared loss function
> 2. **Consensus trajectory**: Methods vote on trajectory points, weighted by local confidence
> 3. **Ensemble Kalman**: Multiple models combined in a Kalman framework
> 4. **Iterative refinement**: Each method refines the prediction, passing results between methods
> 5. **Multi-objective optimization**: Pareto-optimal solution combining all method objectives
> 6. **Gradient boosting style**: Sequential correction where each method corrects previous errors
> 7. **Bayesian hierarchical model**: All methods as observations of a latent true trajectory
> 
> Let me implement several of these novel fusion approaches alongside the existing ones.
> 
> For the "true joint fitting" - I think the key insight the user wants is:
> - NOT: run 6 methods → pick best
> - BUT: 6 methods simultaneously contribute to shaping one optimal trajectory
> 
> Approaches to true joint fitting:
> 
> **Approach A: Weighted Trajectory Averaging (WTA)**
> Each method proposes a full trajectory. The fused trajectory is a pointwise weighted average, where weights depend on local confidence of each method.
> 
> **Approach B: Consensus Point Cloud (CPC)**
> Each method generates multiple plausible trajectories (Monte Carlo within its uncertainty). All points from all methods form a point cloud. Fit a smooth curve through the densest regions.
> 
> **Approach C: Iterative Mutual Refinement (IMR)**
> Start with method A's prediction. Method B refines it using physical constraints. Method C adjusts using spline smoothness. Iterate until convergence.
> 
> **Approach D: Global Joint Optimization (GJO)**
> Define one trajectory parameterization. All methods contribute terms to a single loss function. Optimize the trajectory to minimize the combined loss.
> 
> **Approach E: Bayesian Stacking**
> Treat each method as a noisy observation of ground truth. Use Bayesian inference to combine, accounting for method correlations.
> 
> Let me implement all of these and compare. This will be very comprehensive.

simulate_shuttlecock_trajectory(m, b_drag, x0, y0, vx0, vy0, dt=0.001, max_t=3.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Full physical simulation of shuttlecock flight.\n    Uses quadratic drag model: F_drag = -b * |v| * v\n    \n    m: mass (kg)\n    b_drag: drag coefficient (kg/m)  \n    x0, y0: initial position (m)\n    vx0, vy0: initial velocity (m/s)\n    dt: time step for simulation\n    max_t: max simulation time\n    \"\"\"\n    g = 9.81\n    # Adaptive dt based on velocity\n    v_mag = np.sqrt(vx0**2 + vy0**2)\n    \n    # Pre-allocate\n    max_steps = int(max_t / dt) + 10\n    xs = np.zeros(max_steps)\n    ys = np.zeros(max_steps)\n    ts = np.zeros(max_steps)\n    \n    x, y = x0, y0\n    vx, vy = vx0, vy0\n    t = 0.0\n    step = 0\n    \n    xs[step], ys[step], ts[step] = x, vx\n        ay = -g - drag_factor * vy\n        \n        # RK4 integration for accuracy\n        # k1\n        k1_vx = ax * dt\n        k1_vy = ay * dt\n        k1_x = vx * dt\n        k1_y = vy * dt\n        \n        # k2\n        vx2 = vx + k1_vx/2\n        vy2 = vy + k1_vy/2\n        v2 = np.sqrt(vx2**2 + vy2**2)\n        drag2 = b_drag * v2 / m\n        k2_vx = (-drag2 * vx2) * dt\n        k2_vy = (-g - drag2 * vy2) * dt\n        k2_x = vx2 * dt\n        k2_y = vy2 * dt\n        \n        # k3\n        vx3 = vx + k2_vx/2\n        vy3 = vy + k2_vy/2\n        v3 = np.sqrt(vx3**2 + vy3**2)\n        drag3 = b_drag * v3 / m\n        k3_vx = (-drag3 * vx3) * dt\n        k3_vy = (-g - drag3 * vy3) * dt\n        k3_x = vx3 * dt\n        k3_y = vy3 * dt\n        \n        # k4\n        vx4 = vx + k3_vx\n        vy4 = vy + k3_vy\n        v4 = np.sqrt(vx4**2 + vy4**2)\n        drag4 = b_drag * v4 / m\n        k4_vx = (-drag4 * vx4) * dt\n        k4_vy = (-g - drag4 * vy4) * dt\n        k4_x = vx4 * dt\n        k4_y = vy4 * dt\n        \n        vx += (k1_vx + 2*k2_vx + 2*k3_vx + k4_vx) / 6\n        vy += (k1_vy + 2*k2_vy + 2*k3_vy + k4_vy) / 6\n        x += (k1_x + 2*k2_x + 2*k3_x + k4_x) / 6\n        y += (k1_y + 2*k2_y + 2*k3_y + k4_y) / 6\n        t += dt\n        \n        step += 1\n        xs[step] = x\n        ys[step] = y\n        ts[step] = t\n        \n        # Stop (xs[step] - xs[step-1])\n            ys[step] = 0.0\n            ts[step] = ts[step-1] + frac * (ts[step] - ts[step-1])\n            break\n    \n    return ts[:step+1], xs[:step+1], distribution.\n    \"\"\"\n    # --- Court geometry ---\n    # Badminton court: 13.4m x 6.1m (singles: 5.18m wide)\n    # Net at x=6.7m from baseline, height 1.55m at posts, 1.524m at center\n    # We simulate in a coordinate system: x = distance from one baseline\n    \n    # --- Shot type selection ---\n    if shot_type is None:\n        shot_type = np.random.choice(['smash', 'clear', 'drop', determines terminal velocity: v_term = sqrt(mg/b)\n    # Typical v_term ~ 6.5-7.2 m/s\n    v_term_target = np.random.normal(6.85, 0.25)\n    v_term_target = np.clip(v_term_target, 6.3, 7.5)\n      speed = np.random.normal(40, 15)\n        speed = np.clip(speed, 10, 80)\n        angle = np.random.normal(5, 12)\n        angle = np.clip(angle, -15, 40)\n    \n    # Convert to components\n    angle_rad = np.radians(angle)\n    vx0 = speed * np.cos(angle_rad)\n    vy0 = speed * np.sin(angle_rad)\n    \n    # --- Add spin perturbation ---\n    # Spin slightly modifies effective drag\n speed, 'angle': angle,\n        'spin_rps': spin_rps,\n        'wind_vx': wind_vx, 'wind_vy': wind_vy,\n        'true_landing_x': true_landing_x,\n        'noise_std_mm': noise_std * 1000,\n - obs_t[0] if len(obs_t) > 1 else 0,\n    }\n    \n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_trajectory_batch(n):\n    \"\"\"Generate n trajectories with full variability.\"\"\"\n    trajectories = []\n    for i in range(n):\n        traj = generate_variable_trajectory()\n        trajectories.append(traj)\n    return trajectories\n\n\nprint(\"Generating 2500 variable trajectories...\")\nN_TRAJECTORIES = 2500\nall_trajs = generate_trajectory_batch(N_TRAJECTORIES)\nprint(f\"Generated {len(all_trajs)} trajectories.\")\n\n# Summary stats\nshot_types = [t['shot_type'] for t in all_trajs]\nspeeds = [t['speed'] for t in all_trajs]\nmasses = [t['m']*1000 for t in all_trajs]\nb_drags = [t['b_drag'] for t in all_trajs]\nnoises = [t['noise_std_mm'] for t in all_trajs]\nn_frames_list = [t['n_frames'] for t in all_trajs]\ntrue_landings = [t['true_landing_x'] for t in all_trajs]\n\nprint(f\"\\n--- Dataset Statistics ---\")\nprint(f\"Shot types: {dict(zip(*np.unique(shot_types, return_counts=True)))}\")\nprint(f\"Speed: {np.mean(speeds):.1f} ± {np.std(speeds):.1f} m/s [{np.min(speeds):.0f}, {np.max(speeds):.0f}]\")\nprint(f\"Mass: {np.mean(masses):.3f} ± {np.std(masses):.3f} g [{np.min(masses):.2f}, {np.max(masses):.2f}]\")\nprint(f\"Drag b: {np.mean(b_drags)*1000:.4f} ± {np.std(b_drags)*1000:.4f} g/m\")\nprint(f\"Noise: {np.mean(noises):.2f} ± {np.std(noises):.2f} mm [{np.min(noises):.2f}, {np.max(noises):.2f}]\")\nprint(f\"Frames: {np.mean(n_frames_list):.1f} ± {np.std(n_frames_list):.1f} [{np.min(n_frames_list)}, {np.max(n_frames_list)}]\")\nprint(f\"Landing X: {np.mean(true_landings):.2f} ± {np.std(true_landings):.2f} m [{np.min(true_landings):.2f}, {np.max(true_landings):.2f}]\")\n\nprint(\"\\nDataset generation complete. Everything varies. Nothing is fixed.\")\n",
    "outputs": [

> **思考过程**
> 
> Now I need to implement all the prediction methods and fusion methods, then run the full statistical validation. This is the core of the work. Let me write it as one large code block.
> 
> I need to implement:
> 
> **6 Individual Methods (M1-M6):**
> 1. M1: Polynomial extrapolation (3rd order fit to observed data, extrapolate to y=0)
> 2. M2: Physical model with FIXED standard parameters (m=5.0g, b=1.0 g/m)
> 3. M3: Physical model with ONLINE fitting (fit m and b from observed data)
> 4. M4: Kalman filter with constant acceleration model
> 5. M5: Cubic spline extrapolation
> 6. M6: Velocity-damping extrapolation (fit exponential decay of velocity, then integrate)
> 
> **8 Fusion Methods:**
> 1. Simple Average (baseline disaster)
> 2. Cross-Scoring Fusion (current best)
> 3. Weighted Average by Fit Quality
> 4. Consensus Point Cloud (RANSAC-like)
> 5. Iterative Mutual Refinement
> 6. Global Joint Optimization
> 7. Bayesian Model Averaging
> 8. Stacking Ensemble (median-based)
> 
> Let me implement all of these carefully.

现在实现全部14种方法（6种独立预测 + 8种融合策略）：

```json
{
  "toolName": "codeExecutor",
  "state": "output-available",
  "input": {
    "code": "\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution\nfrom scipy.interpolate import UnivariateSpline, CubicSpline\nfrom scipy.stats import norm\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# ============================================================\n# SECTION 2: ALL PREDICTION & FUSION METHODS\n# ============================================================\n\n# --- Physical Constants ---\nG = 9.81\n\n# ==========================================\n# 6 INDIVIDUAL PREDICTION METHODS\n# ==========================================\n\ndef predict_polynomial(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, degree=3):\n    \"\"\"M1: Polynomial extrapolation. Fit polynomial to observed (t,x) and (t,y), extrapolate to y=0.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Fit x(t) and y(t) as polynomials\n        coeffs_x = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_x, min(degree, len(obs_t)-1))\n        coeffs_y = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_y, min(degree, len(obs_t)-1))\n        \n        # Find t where y(t) = 0\n        poly_y = np.poly1d(coeffs_y)\n        poly_x = np.poly1d(coeffs_x)\n        \n        # Find roots of y(t)\n        roots = np.roots(coeffs_y)\n        real_roots = roots[np.isreal(roots)].real\n        \n        # Find the first root after the last observation time\n        candidates = real_roots[real_roots > obs_t[-1]]\n        if len(candidates) > 0:\n            t_land = min(candidates)\n            x_land = poly_x(t_land)\n            if 0 < x_land < 20:\n                return x_land\n        \n        # Fallback: extrapolate step by step\n        dt = 0.005\n        t = obs_t[-1]\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        vx = np.polyval(np.polyder(coeffs_x), t)\n        vy = np.polyval(np.polyder(coeffs_y), t)\n        \n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = - (y - vy*dt) / (vy*dt + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                return x - vx*dt*(1-frac)\n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n            t += dt\n            vx = np.polyval(np.polyder(coeffs_x), t)\n            vy = np.polyval(np.polyder(coeffs_y), t)\n        \n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0  # wild guess\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef predict_physical_fixed(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"M2: Physical model with FIXED standard parameters (m=5.0g, b=1.0 g/m).\"\"\"\n    m = 0.0050  # kg\n    b = 0.0010  # kg/m\n    \n    try:\n        # Fit initial conditions from last few observed points\n        dt_obs = obs_t[-1] - obs_t[-2] if len(obs_t) > 1 else 0.01\n        vx_obs = (obs_x[-1] - obs_x[-2]) / dt_obs if len(obs_t) > 1 else 20\n        vy_obs = (obs_y[-1] - obs_y[-2]) / dt_obs if len(obs_t) > 1 else -5\n        \n        # Simulate forward\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        vx, vy = vx_obs, vy_obs\n        dt = 0.001\n        \n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = -y / ((y + vy*dt) - y + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                return x + vx * dt * (1 - frac)\n            v = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n            drag = b * v / m\n            ax = -drag * vx\n            ay = -G - drag * vy\n            vx += ax * dt\n            vy += ay * dt\n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n        \n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef predict_physical_online(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"M3: Physical model with ONLINE drag fitting from observed data.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Estimate velocities from observations\n        dts = np.diff(obs_t)\n        vxs = np.diff(obs_x) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n        vys = np.diff(obs_y) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n        \n        t_mid = obs_t[:-1] + dts/2\n        x_mid = obs_x[:-1] + np.diff(obs_x)/2\n        y_mid = obs_y[:-1] + np.diff(obs_y)/2\n        \n        # Estimate accelerations\n        if len(vxs) > 2:\n            axs = np.gradient(vxs, dts)\n            ays = np.gradient(vys, dts)\n            \n            # ax = -b/m * v * vx → b/m = -ax / (v * vx)\n            # ay = -g -b/m * v * vy → b/m = -(ay+g) / (v*vy)\n            vs = np.sqrt(vxs**2 + vys**2)\n            \n            # Estimate b/m from horizontal and vertical components\n            b_over_m_x = -axs / np.clip(vs * vxs, 1e-3, None)\n            b_over_m_y = -(ays + G) / np.clip(vs * vys, 1e-3, None)\n            \n            # Weight by reliability (horizontal more reliable when vx is large)\n            weights_x = np.clip(np.abs(vxs), 0.5, None)\n            weights_y = np.clip(np.abs(vys), 0.5, None)\n            \n            b_over_m_valid_x = b_over_m_x[(b_over_m_x > 0) & (b_over_m_x < 1000)]\n            b_over_m_valid_y = b_over_m_y[(b_over_m_y > 0) & (b_over_m_y < 1000)]\n            \n            if len(b_over_m_valid_x) > 0 or len(b_over_m_valid_y) > 0:\n                all_valid = np.concatenate([b_over_m_valid_x, b_over_m_valid_y])\n                b_over_m = np.median(all_valid)\n            else:\n                b_over_m = 200.0  # default ~ 0.001/0.005\n        else:\n            b_over_m = 200.0\n        \n        b_over_m = np.clip(b_over_m, 50, 500)\n        \n        # Simulate forward with fitted drag\n        vx = vxs[-1] if len(vxs) > 0 else 20\n        vy = vys[-1] if len(vys) > 0 else -5\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        dt = 0.001\n        \n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = -y / ((y + vy*dt) - y + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                return x + vx * dt * (1 - frac)\n            v = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n            drag = b_over_m * v\n            ax = -drag * vx\n            ay = -G - drag * vy\n            vx += ax * dt\n            vy += ay * dt\n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n        \n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef predict_kalman_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"M4: Kalman filter with near-constant-velocity model, then extrapolate.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 3:\n            return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        \n        # Simple Kalman: estimate smoothed velocity at last point\n        # Use triple exponential smoothing on position to get velocity\n        dt_avg = np.mean(np.diff(obs_t))\n        \n        # Smooth the data first\n        from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter1d\n        sigma = max(1.0, n / 30.0)\n        xs_smooth = gaussian_filter1d(obs_x, sigma=sigma)\n        ys_smooth = gaussian_filter1d(obs_y, sigma=sigma)\n        \n        # Estimate velocity at end\n        if n >= 4:\n            vx = (xs_smooth[-1] - xs_smooth[-4]) / (obs_t[-1] - obs_t[-4])\n            vy = (ys_smooth[-1] - ys_smooth[-4]) / (obs_t[-1] - obs_t[-4])\n        else:\n            vx = (xs_smooth[-1] - xs_smooth[0]) / (obs_t[-1] - obs_t[0])\n            vy = (ys_smooth[-1] - ys_smooth[0]) / (obs_t[-1] - obs_t[0])\n        \n        # Extrapolate with constant velocity (simplified)\n        if vy >= 0:\n            vy = -2.0  # force downward\n        \n        time_to_ground = -obs_y[-1] / vy if vy < 0 else 2.0\n        return obs_x[-1] + vx * time_to_ground\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef predict_spline_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"M5: Cubic spline extrapolation with damping.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 4:\n            return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        \n        # Fit splines\n        k = min(3, n-1)\n        spline_x = UnivariateSpline(obs_t, obs_x, k=k, s=0.5)\n        spline_y = UnivariateSpline(obs_t, obs_y, k=k, s=0.5)\n        \n        # Extrapolate\n        dt = 0.002\n        t = obs_t[-1]\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        \n        for _ in range(3000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = -y / ((y + spline_y.derivative()(t)*dt) - y + 1e-12)\n                frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                return x + spline_x.derivative()(t) * dt * (1 - frac)\n            t += dt\n            vx = spline_x.derivative()(t) if t <= obs_t[-1] + 0.5 else spline_x.derivative()(obs_t[-1] + 0.5)\n            vy = spline_y.derivative()(t) if t <= obs_t[-1] + 0.5 else spline_y.derivative()(obs_t[-1] + 0.5)\n            \n            # Apply some damping for realism\n            damping = np.exp(-0.3 * (t - obs_t[-1]))\n            x += vx * dt * damping\n            y += vy * dt * damping\n            if y > 100 or x > 30:\n                break\n        \n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef predict_velocity_damping(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"M6: Fit exponential decay to velocity magnitude, then integrate to landing.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 3:\n            return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        \n        # Compute velocity magnitudes\n        dts = np.diff(obs_t)\n        vxs = np.diff(obs_x) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n        vys = np.diff(obs_y) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n        speeds = np.sqrt(vxs**2 + vys**2)\n        angles = np.arctan2(vys, vxs)\n        t_mid = obs_t[:-1] + dts/2\n        \n        # Fit exponential decay to speed: v(t) = v0 * exp(-k*t) + v_term\n        # Approximate by fitting log(speed)\n        if len(speeds) > 3:\n            # Simple linear fit to log(speed - v_term)\n            v_term_guess = 6.5\n            log_speeds = np.log(np.clip(speeds - v_term_guess, 0.5, None))\n            valid = np.isfinite(log_speeds)\n            if np.sum(valid) > 2:\n                coeffs = np.polyfit(t_mid[valid], log_speeds[valid], 1)\n                k = -coeffs[0]\n                v0 = np.exp(coeffs[1]) + v_term_guess\n            else:\n                k = 1.5\n                v0 = speeds[0]\n        else:\n            k = 1.5\n            v0 = speeds[0]\n        \n        k = np.clip(k, 0.2, 10.0)\n        v0 = np.clip(v0, 5, 100)\n        \n        # Integrate forward\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        vx = vxs[-1] if len(vxs) > 0 else v0 * np.cos(np.mean(angles))\n        vy = vys[-1] if len(vys) > 0 else -v0 * np.sin(np.abs(np.mean(angles)))\n        t = obs_t[-1]\n        dt = 0.001\n        t0 = obs_t[0]\n        \n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = -y / ((y + vy*dt) - y + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                return x + vx * dt * (1 - frac)\n            \n            # Exponential decay of speed\n            speed = v0 * np.exp(-k * (t - t0)) + 6.5\n            current_speed = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n            \n            # Adjust velocity direction slightly toward terminal\n            if current_speed > 1e-6:\n                # Maintain direction but adjust magnitude\n                vx_dir = vx / current_speed\n                vy_dir = vy / current_speed\n                \n                # target speed from exponential fit\n                target_speed = v0 * np.exp(-k * (t - t0)) + 6.5\n                \n                # Smooth toward target\n                blend = 0.05\n                new_speed = (1-blend) * current_speed + blend * target_speed\n                \n                vx = vx_dir * new_speed\n                vy = vy_dir * new_speed\n            \n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n            t += dt\n            \n            # Gravity still acts\n            vy -= G * dt * 0.3  # reduced gravity effect due to drag\n        \n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\n# ==========================================\n# 8 FUSION STRATEGIES\n# ==========================================\n\ndef fusion_simple_average(predictions):\n    \"\"\"F1: Simple average of all predictions.\"\"\"\n    return np.mean(predictions)\n\n\ndef fusion_trimmed_mean(predictions, trim_frac=0.25):\n    \"\"\"F1b: Trimmed mean - discard outliers.\"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    n_trim = int(n * trim_frac)\n    if n_trim == 0:\n        return np.mean(predictions)\n    sorted_preds = np.sort(predictions)\n    return np.mean(sorted_preds[n_trim:-n_trim])\n\n\ndef fusion_cross_scoring(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"F2: Cross-scoring fusion. Each method scores others, highest total wins.\"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    scores = np.zeros(n)\n    \n    for i in range(n):  # judge\n        for j in range(n):  # proposal\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n            \n            # Judge i evaluates proposal j\n            # Score based on consistency with method i's model\n            \n            # 1. Physical consistency score\n            # How well does proposal j fit method i's trajectory model?\n            # For simplicity, use distance-based consistency\n            diff_from_i = abs(predictions[j] - predictions[i])\n            \n            # Expected tolerance based on data quality\n            n_frames = len(obs_t)\n            tolerance = 0.3 + 3.0 / np.sqrt(n_frames)  # tighter with more data\n            \n            consistency = np.exp(-diff_from_i**2 / (2 * tolerance**2))\n            scores[j] += consistency * 10.0  # weight\n    \n    # Add self-consistency bonus (fit quality to own model)\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Method fits itself perfectly by definition, so this is a baseline\n        scores[i] += 2.0\n    \n    # Winner takes all\n    winner = np.argmax(scores)\n    return predictions[winner], scores, winner\n\n\ndef fusion_weighted_average(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"F3: Weighted average by local fit quality.\"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    weights = np.ones(n)\n    \n    # Weight based on agreement with consensus\n    median_pred = np.median(predictions)\n    mad = np.median(np.abs(predictions - median_pred))\n    if mad < 1e-6:\n        mad = 0.1\n    \n    for i in range(n):\n        # Weight inversely proportional to distance from median\n        dist = abs(predictions[i] - median_pred)\n        weights[i] = np.exp(-dist**2 / (2 * (mad * 1.4826)**2))\n    \n    weights = weights / np.sum(weights)\n    return np.sum(predictions * weights)\n\n\ndef fusion_consensus_ransac(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=100):\n    \"\"\"F4: RANSAC-like consensus. Find largest agreeing subset.\"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    if n < 3:\n        return np.median(predictions)\n    \n    best_consensus = 0\n    best_center = np.median(predictions)\n    \n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        # Random pair\n        idx = np.random.choice(n, min(3, n), replace=False)\n        center = np.mean(predictions[idx])\n        \n        # Count inliers\n        inliers = np.sum(np.abs(predictions - center) < 0.5)\n        \n        if inliers > best_consensus:\n            best_consensus = inliers\n            best_center = center\n    \n    # Return mean of inliers\n    inlier_mask = np.abs(predictions - best_center) < 0.5\n    if np.sum(inlier_mask) >= 2:\n        return np.mean(predictions[inlier_mask])\n    else:\n        return np.median(predictions)\n\n\ndef fusion_iterative_refinement(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=5):\n    \"\"\"F5: Iterative mutual refinement. Methods refine each other's predictions.\"\"\"\n    refined = predictions.copy().astype(float)\n    \n    for iteration in range(n_iter):\n        # Each method pulls others toward consensus\n        new_refined = refined.copy()\n        for i in range(len(predictions)):\n            # Weighted pull from other methods\n            pull = 0\n            total_weight = 0\n            for j in range(len(predictions)):\n                if i == j:\n                    continue\n                # Weight based on agreement\n                diff = abs(refined[i] - refined[j])\n                weight = np.exp(-diff**2 / 0.5)\n                pull += refined[j] * weight\n                total_weight += weight\n            \n            if total_weight > 0:\n                # Move toward consensus\n                consensus = pull / total_weight\n                new_refined[i] = 0.7 * refined[i] + 0.3 * consensus\n        \n        refined = new_refined\n    \n    # Return the converged consensus\n    return np.mean(refined)\n\n\ndef fusion_global_joint_optimization(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"F6: Global Joint Optimization - truly fitting all models simultaneously.\n    \n    This is the key \"共同拟合\" method: instead of selecting among methods,\n    we define a single parameterized trajectory and optimize it against\n    ALL method objectives simultaneously.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 3:\n            return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        \n        # Parameterize trajectory: initial position + initial velocity + drag\n        # θ = [x_last, y_last, vx, vy, b_over_m]\n        # We optimize to minimize combined loss from all methods\n        \n        x0_vec = np.array([obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]])\n        \n        # Initial velocity estimate\n        dt_last = obs_t[-1] - obs_t[-2] if n > 1 else 0.01\n        vx_init = (obs_x[-1] - obs_x[-2]) / dt_last if n > 1 else 20\n        vy_init = (obs_y[-1] - obs_y[-2]) / dt_last if n > 1 else -5\n        \n        # Initial b/m estimate (from M3 logic)\n        if n > 3:\n            dts = np.diff(obs_t)\n            vxs = np.diff(obs_x) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n            vys = np.diff(obs_y) / np.clip(dts, 1e-6, None)\n            vs = np.sqrt(vxs**2 + vys**2)\n            axs = np.gradient(vxs, dts)\n            b_over_m_init = np.median(-axs / np.clip(vs * vxs, 1e-3, None))\n            b_over_m_init = np.clip(b_over_m_init, 50, 500) if np.isfinite(b_over_m_init) else 200\n        else:\n            b_over_m_init = 200\n        \n        def forward_sim(params):\n            \"\"\"Simulate trajectory to landing given initial state and b/m.\"\"\"\n            x, y = params[0], params[1]\n            vx, vy = params[2], params[3]\n            bom = params[4]\n            \n            dt = 0.001\n            for _ in range(5000):\n                if y <= 0:\n                    frac = -y / ((y + vy*dt) - y + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                    frac = np.clip(frac, 0, 1)\n                    return x + vx * dt * (1 - frac)\n                v = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n                drag = bom * v\n                vx += (-drag * vx) * dt\n                vy += (-G - drag * vy) * dt\n                x += vx * dt\n                y += vy * dt\n            return x\n        \n        def combined_loss(params):\n            \"\"\"Loss function combining multiple objectives.\"\"\"\n            x0, y0, vx0, vy0, bom = params\n            \n            # 1. Data fidelity: simulate backward from last point\n            # Compare with observed trajectory\n            data_loss = 0\n            x, y = x0, y0\n            vx, vy = vx0, vy0\n            \n            # Simulate backward\n            dt = 0.001\n            t = obs_t[-1]\n            for i in range(len(obs_t)-2, -1, -1):\n                target_t = obs_t[i]\n                while t > target_t:\n                    v = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n                    drag = bom * v\n                    vx -= (-drag * vx) * dt  # backward = reverse forces\n                    vy -= (-G - drag * vy) * dt\n                    x -= vx * dt\n                    y -= vy * dt\n                    t -= dt\n                data_loss += (x - obs_x[i])**2 + (y - obs_y[i])**2\n            \n            # 2. Physical plausibility\n            # b/m should be reasonable\n            phys_loss = ((bom - 200) / 100)**2\n            \n            # 3. Smoothness (velocity should be consistent)\n            v_init = np.sqrt(vx0**2 + vy0**2)\n            \n            return data_loss / max(len(obs_t), 1) + 0.1 * phys_loss\n        \n        # Optimize\n        initial_params = [obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1], vx_init, vy_init, b_over_m_init]\n        bounds = [\n            (obs_x[-1] - 2, obs_x[-1] + 2),\n            (obs_y[-1] - 1, obs_y[-1] + 1),\n            (-50, 80),\n            (-60, 20),\n            (50, 500)\n        ]\n        \n        result = minimize(\n            combined_loss, initial_params,\n            method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,\n            options={'maxiter': 100}\n        )\n        \n        if result.success:\n            return forward_sim(result.x)\n        else:\n            return forward_sim(initial_params)\n            \n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\n\ndef fusion_bayesian_averaging(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"F7: Bayesian Model Averaging.\n    Each method gets a posterior weight based on how well it explains the data.\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    \n    # Compute prior: uniform\n    prior = np.ones(n) / n\n    \n    # Compute likelihood: how consistent is each prediction with observed data?\n    # Methods that produce predictions consistent with local trajectory get higher weight\n    likelihoods = np.ones(n)\n    \n    median_pred = np.median(predictions)\n    \n    for i in range(n):\n        # Distance from median as proxy for consistency\n        dist = abs(predictions[i] - median_pred)\n        # Also consider physical range\n        if predictions[i] < 0 or predictions[i] > 15:\n            likelihoods[i] = 0.01\n        else:\n            likelihoods[i] = np.exp(-dist**2 / 2.0)\n    \n    # Posterior = prior * likelihood\n    posterior = prior * likelihoods\n    posterior = posterior / np.sum(posterior)\n    \n    # Weighted average\n    return np.sum(predictions * posterior)\n\n\ndef fusion_stacking_ensemble(predictions, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    \"\"\"F8: Stacking ensemble with robust statistics.\n    Uses median, trimmed mean, and outlier rejection in cascade.\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(predictions)\n    \n    # Step 1: Compute robust center\n    median = np.median(predictions)\n    \n    # Step 2: Compute robust scale (MAD)\n    mad = np.median(np.abs(predictions - median)) * 1.4826\n    if mad < 0.05:\n        mad = 0.05\n    \n    # Step 3: Reject outliers (> 3 MAD from median)\n    inlier_mask = np.abs(predictions - median) < 3 * mad\n    \n    if np.sum(inlier_mask) >= 2:\n        # Use mean of inliers, weighted by proximity to median\n        inlier_preds = predictions[inlier_mask]\n        dists = np.abs(inlier_preds - median)\n        weights = np.exp(-dists**2 / (2 * mad**2))\n        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)\n        return np.sum(inlier_preds * weights)\n    else:\n        return median\n\n\n# ==========================================\n# RUN ALL METHODS ON ONE TRAJECTORY\n# ==========================================\n\nMETHOD_NAMES = ['M1_Poly', 'M2_PhysFixed', 'M3_PhysOnline', 'M4_Kalman', 'M5_Spline', 'M6_VelDamping']\nFUSION_NAMES = ['F1_SimpleAvg', 'F2_CrossScoring', 'F3_WeightedAvg', 'F4_ConsensusRANSAC',\n                'F5_IterativeRefine', 'F6_JointOptimize', 'F7_BayesianAvg', 'F8_StackingEnsemble']\n\ndef run_all_methods(traj):\n    \"\"\"Run all 6 individual methods + 8 fusion methods on one trajectory.\"\"\"\n    obs_t = traj['obs_t']\n    obs_x = traj['obs_x']\n    obs_y = traj['obs_y']\n    true_x = traj['true_landing_x']\n    \n    # Run individual methods\n    preds = np.array([\n        predict_polynomial(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n        predict_physical_fixed(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n        predict_physical_online(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n        predict_kalman_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n        predict_spline_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n        predict_velocity_damping(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y),\n    ])\n    \n    errors = np.abs(preds - true_x)\n    \n    # Run fusion methods\n    fusion_preds = np.zeros(8)\n    \n    fusion_preds[0] = fusion_simple_average(preds)\n    fusion_preds[1], _, _ = fusion_cross_scoring(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[2] = fusion_weighted_average(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[3] = fusion_consensus_ransac(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[4] = fusion_iterative_refinement(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[5] = fusion_global_joint_optimization(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[6] = fusion_bayesian_averaging(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    fusion_preds[7] = fusion_stacking_ensemble(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y)\n    \n    fusion_errors = np.abs(fusion_preds - true_x)\n    \n    return preds, errors, fusion_preds, fusion_errors\n\n\nprint(\"All methods defined. Ready for massive validation.\")\nprint(f\"Individual methods: {METHOD_NAMES}\")\nprint(f\"Fusion methods: {FUSION_NAMES}\")\n"
  },
  "output": {

warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Re-define all methods (needed in new sandbox)\nG = 9.81\n\ndef predict_polynomial(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, degree=3):\n    try:\n        coeffs_x = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_x, min(degree, len(obs_t)-1))\n        coeffs_y = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_y, min(degree, len(obs_t)-1))\n        poly_y = np.poly1d(coeffs_y)\n        poly_x =            vy += (-G - drag * vy) * dt\n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\ndef predict_physical_online(obs_t, obs_x, vys**2)\n        if len(vxs) > 2:\n            axs = np.gradient(vxs, dts)\n            b_over_m_x = -axs / np.clip(vs * vxs, 1e-3, None)\n            b_over_m_valid = b_over_m_x[(b_over_m_x > 0) & (b_over_m_x < 1000)]\n            b_over_m = np.median(b_over_m_valid) if len(b_over_m_valid) > 0 else 200\n        else:\n            b_over_m = 200\n        b_over_m = np.clip(b_over_m, 50, 500)\n        vx = vxs[-1] if len(vxs) > 0 else 20\n        vy = vys[-1] if len(vys) > 0 else -5\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        dt = 0.001\n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                frac = -y / ((y + vy*dt) - y + 1e-12) if vy < 0 else 0\n                return x + vx * dt * (1 - np.clip(frac,0,1))\n            v = np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2)\n            drag = b_over_m * v\n            vx += (-drag * vx) * dt\n            vy += (-G - drag * vy) * dt\n            x += vx * dt\n            y += vy * dt\n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\ndef predict_kalman_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 3: return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        sigma = max(1.0, n/30.0)\n        xs_s = gaussian_filter1d(obs_x, sigma=sigma)\n        ys_s = gaussian_filter1d(obs_y, sigma=sigma)\n        if n >= 4:\n            vx = (xs_s[-1]-xs_s[-4])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-4])\n            vy = (ys_s[-1]-ys_s[-4])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-4])\n        else:\n            vx = (xs_s[-1]-xs_s[0])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[0])\n            vy = (ys_s[-1]-ys_s[0])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[0])\n        if vy >= 0: vy = -2.0\n        return obs_x[-1] + vx * (-obs_y[-1]/vy)\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\ndef predict_spline_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 4: return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        k = min(3, n-1)\n        sp_x = UnivariateSpline(obs_t, obs_x, k=k, s=0.5)\n        sp_y = UnivariateSpline(obs_t, obs_y, k=k, s=0.5)\n        dt = 0.002\n        t = obs_t[-1]\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]\n        for _ in range(3000):\n            if y <= 0:\n                return x\n            t += dt\n            vx = sp_x.derivative()(min(t, obs_t[-1]+0.5))\n            vy = sp_y.derivative()(min(t, obs_t[-1]+0.5))\n            damp = np.exp(-0.3*(t-obs_t[-1]))\n            x += vx*dt*damp\n            y += vy*dt*damp\n            if y > 100 or x > 30: break\n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\ndef predict_velocity_damping(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n = len(obs_t)\n        if n < 3: return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n        dts = np.diff(obs_t)\n        vxs = np.diff(obs_x)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n        vys = np.diff(obs_y)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n        speeds = np.sqrt(vxs**2+vys**2)\n        t_mid = obs_t[:-1]+dts/2\n        if len(speeds)>3:\n            v_term = 6.5\n            log_s = np.log(np.clip(speeds-v_term,0.5,None))\n            valid=np.isfinite(log_s)\n            if np.sum(valid)>2:\n                c=np.polyfit(t_mid[valid],log_s[valid],1)\n                k=-c[0]\n            else:\n                k=1.5\n        else:\n            k=1.5\n        k=np.clip(k,0.2,10)\n        vx=vxs[-1]; vy=vys[-1]; x=obs_x[-1]; y=obs_y[-1]; t=obs_t[-1]; dt=0.001\n        for _ in range(5000):\n            if y<=0:\n                frac=-y/((y+vy*dt)-y+1e-12) if vy<0 else 0\n                return x+vx*dt*(1-np.clip(frac,0,1))\n            sp=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2)\n            if sp>1e-6:\n                vx_d=vx/sp; vy_d=vy/sp\n                tgt=speeds[0]*np.exp(-k*(t-obs_t[0]))+6.5\n                new_sp=0.95*sp+0.05*tgt\n                vx=vx_d*new_sp; vy=vy_d*new_sp\n            x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt; t+=dt\n            vy-=G*dt*0.3\n        return x\n    except:\n        return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\ndef fusion_simple_average(preds):\n    return np.mean(preds)\n\ndef fusion_cross_scoring(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    n=len(preds); scores=np.zeros(n)\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(n):\n            if i==j: continue\n            diff=abs(preds[j]-preds[i])\n            tol=0.3+3.0/np.sqrt(max(len(obs_t),1))\n            scores[j]+=np.exp(-diff**2/(2*tol**2))*10.0\n    for i in range(n): scores[i]+=2.0\n    return preds[np.argmax(scores)]\n\ndef fusion_weighted_average(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    med=np.median(preds); mad=np.median(np.abs(preds-med))*1.4826\n    if mad<0.01: mad=0.01\n    w=np.exp(-(preds-med)**2/(2*mad**2))\n    return np.sum(preds*w)/np.sum(w)\n\ndef fusion_consensus_ransac(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=100):\n    n=len(preds)\n    if n<3: return np.median(preds)\n    best_c=0; best_x=np.median(preds)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        idx=np.random.choice(n, min(3,n), replace=False)\n        c=np.mean(preds[idx])\n        inl=np.sum(np.abs(preds-c)<0.5)\n        if inl>best_c: best_c=inl; best_x=c\n    mask=np.abs(preds-best_x)<0.5\n    return np.mean(preds[mask]) if np.sum(mask)>=2 else np.median(preds)\n\ndef fusion_iterative_refinement(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=5):\n    r=preds.copy().astype(float)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        nr=r.copy()\n        for i in range(len(preds)):\n            pull=0; tw=0\n            for j in range(len(preds)):\n                if i==j: continue\n                w=np.exp(-(r[i]-r[j])**2/0.5)\n                pull+=r[j]*w; tw+=w\n            if tw>0: nr[i]=0.7*r[i]+0.3*pull/tw\n        r=nr\n    return np.mean(r)\n\ndef fusion_global_joint_optimization(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n=len(obs_t)\n        if n<3: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n        dt_l=obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-2] if n>1 else 0.01\n        vxi=(obs_x[-1]-obs_x[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else 20\n        vyi=(obs_y[-1]-obs_y[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else -5\n        if n>3:\n            dts=np.diff(obs_t); vxs=np.diff(obs_x)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n            break\n    return ts[:step+1],xs[:step+1],ys[:step+1]\n\ndef generate_variable_trajectory(shot_type=None):\n    if shot_type is None:\n speed=np.clip(speed,40,70)\n        angle=np.random.normal(-5,5); angle=np.clip(angle,-15,0)\n    elif shot_type=='clear':\n        speed=np.random.normal(60,8); speed=np.clip(speed,40,75)\n        angle=np.random.normal(25,8); angle=np.clip(angle,10,45)\n    elif shot_type=='drop':\n        speed=np.random.normal(30,7); speed=np.clip(speed,15,45)\n        angle=np.random.normal(5,8); speed=np.random.normal(40,15); speed=np.clip(speed,10,80)\n        angle=np.random.normal(5,12); angle=np.clip(angle,-15,40)\n    \n    angle_rad=np.radians(angle)\n    vx0=speed*np.cos(angle_rad); vy0=speed*np.sin(angle_rad)\n    wind_vx=np.random.normal(0,0.3); wind_vy=np.random.normal(0,0.15)\n    ts,xs,ys=simulate_shuttlecock_trajectory(m,b_drag,x0,y0,vx0+wind_vx,vy0+wind_vy)\n    \n    landing_idx=np.where(ys<=0)[0]\n    if len(landing_idx)>0:\n        land_idx=landing_idx[0]\n        if land_idx>0:\n            frac=-ys[land_idx-1]/(ys[land_idx]-ys[land_idx-1]+1e-12)\n if len(obs_t)>1 else 0}\n\nprint(\"Regenerating 2500 trajectories...\")\nN=2500\nall_trajs=[generate_variable_trajectory() for _ in range(N)]\nprint(f\"Done. {N} trajectories × 14 methods in {elapsed:.1f}s ({elapsed/N*1000:.1f}ms per trajectory)\")\n\n# Save results\nimport pickle\nresults={'method_errors':all_method_errors,'fusion_errors':all_fusion_errors,\n         'method_names':METHOD_NAMES,'fusion_names':FUSION_NAMES,\n         'trajectories':all_trajs,'n_trajectories':N}\nnp.savez('/output/validation_results.npz',method_errors=all_method_errors,fusion_errors=all_fusion_errors)\n\nprint(\"\\nResults saved. > **思考过程**
> 
> The sandbox timed out. 2500 trajectories × 14 methods is too much for a single execution. Let me reduce the batch size. I'll do 1000 trajectories instead, which should complete within the timeout. Also, I can optimize the code to be faster.
> 
> Let me try with 1000 trajectories first. Also, I should simplify some of the more expensive methods.

沙箱超时了。我优化代码，减少到1000条（仍然是统计学有效的大样本），并加速每种方法：

d = min(degree, n-1)\n        cx = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_x, d); cy = np.polyfit(obs_t, obs_y, d)\n        py = np.poly1d(cy); px = np.poly1d(cx)\n        roots = np.roots(cy); real_roots = roots[np.isreal(roots)].real\n        candidates = real_roots[real_roots > obs_t[-1]]\n        if len(candidates) > 0:\n            t_land = min(candidates); t += dt\n        return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n    except: return obs_x[-1] + 2.0\n\ndef predict_physical_fixed(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    m, b = 0.0050, 0.0010\n    try:\n        dt_obs = obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-2] if len(obs_t)>1 else 0.01\n        vx = (obs_x[-1]-obs_x[-2])/dt_obs if len(obs_t)>1 else 20\n        vy = (obs_y[-1]-obs_y[-2])/dt_obs if len(obs_t)>1 else -5\n        x, y = obs_x[-1], obs_y[-1]; drag=bom*v\n            vx+=(-drag*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-drag*vy)*dt\n            x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt\n        return x\n    except: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\ndef predict_kalman_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n=len(obs_t)\n        if n<3: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n        sigma=max(1.0,n/30.0)\n        xs=gaussian_filter1d(obs_x,sigma=sigma); ys=gaussian_filter1d(obs_y,sigma=sigma)\n        if n>=4:\n            vx=(xs[-1]-xs[-4])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-4])\n            vy=(ys[-1]-ys[-4])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-4])\n        else:\n            vx=(xs[-1]-xs[0])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[0])\n            vy=(ys[-1]-ys[0])/(obs_t[-1]-obs_t[0])\n        if vy>=0: vy=-2.0\n        return obs_x[-1]+vx*(-obs_y[-1]/vy)\n    except: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\ndef predict_spline_extrapolation(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n=len(obs_t)\n        if n<4: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n        k=min(3,n-1); sp_x=UnivariateSpline(obs_t,obs_x,k=k,s=0.5)\n        sp_y=UnivariateSpline(obs_t,obs_y,k=k,s=0.5)\n        dt=0.005; t=obs_t[-1]; x,y=obs_x[-1],obs_y[-1]\n        for _ in range(1200):\n            if y<=0:\n                vy=sp_y.derivative()(min(t,obs_t[-1]+0.5))\n                return x if vy>=0 else x - y*sp_x.derivative()(min(t,obs_t[-1]+0.5))/vy\n            t+=dt; damp=np.exp(-0.3*(t-obs_t[-1]))\n            x+=sp_x.derivative()(min(t,obs_t[-1]+0.5))*dt*damp\n            y+=sp_y.derivative()(min(t,obs_t[-1]+0.5))*dt*damp\n            if y>100 or x>30: break\n        return x\n    except: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\ndef predict_velocity_damping(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n=len(obs_t)\n        if n<3: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n        dts=np.diff(obs_t); vxs=np.diff(obs_x)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n        vys=np.diff(obs_y)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n        speeds=np.sqrt(vxs**2+vys**2); t_mid=obs_t[:-1]+dts/2\n        if len(speeds)>3:\n            log_s=np.log(np.clip(speeds-6.5,0.5,None)); vyd=vy/sp\n                tgt=speeds[0]*np.exp(-k*(t-obs_t[0]))+6.5\n                new_sp=0.95*sp+0.05*tgt\n                vx=vxd*new_sp; vy=vyd*new_sp\n            x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt; t+=dt; vy-=G*dt*0.3\n        return x\n    except: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\n# ===== FUSION METHODS =====\n\ndef fusion_simple_average(preds): return np.mean(preds)\n\ndef fusion_cross_scoring(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    n=len(preds); scores=np.zeros(n)\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(n):\n            if i==j: continue\n            diff=abs(preds[j]-preds[i])\n            tol=0.3+3.0/np.sqrt(max(len(obs_t),1))\n            scores[j]+=np.exp(-diff**2/(2*tol**2))*10.0\n    scores+=2.0\n    return preds[np.argmax(scores)]\n\ndef fusion_weighted_average(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    med=np.median(preds); mad=np.median(np.abs(preds-med))*1.4826\n    if mad<0.01: mad=0.01\n    w=np.exp(-(preds-med)**2/(2*mad**2)); w/=w.sum()\n    return np.sum(preds*w)\n\ndef fusion_consensus_ransac(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=50):\n    n=len(preds)\n    if n<3: return np.median(preds)\n    best_c=0; best_x=np.median(preds)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        idx=np.random.choice(n,min(3,n),replace=False)\n        c=np.mean(preds[idx]); inl=np.sum(np.abs(preds-c)<0.5)\n        if inl>best_c: best_c=inl; best_x=c\n    mask=np.abs(preds-best_x)<0.5\n    return np.mean(preds[mask]) if np.sum(mask)>=2 else np.median(preds)\n\ndef fusion_iterative_refinement(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y, n_iter=5):\n    r=preds.copy().astype(float)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        nr=r.copy()\n        for i in range(len(preds)):\n            pull=0; tw=0\n            for j in range(len(preds)):\n                if i==j: continue\n                w=np.exp(-(r[i]-r[j])**2/0.5); pull+=r[j]*w; tw+=w\n            if tw>0: nr[i]=0.7*r[i]+0.3*pull/tw\n        r=nr\n    return np.mean(r)\n\ndef fusion_global_joint_optimization(obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    try:\n        n=len(obs_t)\n        if n<3: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n        dt_l=obs_t[-1]-obs_t[-2] if n>1 else 0.01\n        vxi=(obs_x[-1]-obs_x[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else 20\n        vyi=(obs_y[-1]-obs_y[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else -5\n        if n>3:\n            dts=np.diff(obs_t); vxs=np.diff(obs_x)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n            axs=np.gradient(vxs,dts); vs=np.sqrt(vxs**2+(np.diff(obs_y)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None))**2)\n            bomi=np.median(-axs/np.clip(vs*vxs,1e-3,None))\n            bomi=np.clip(bomi,50,500) if np.isfinite(bomi) else 200\n        else: bomi=200\n        \n        def fwd(p):\n            x,y,vx,vy,bom=p; dt=0.002\n            for _ in range(2500):\n                if y<=0:\n                    frac=-y/(vy*dt+1e-12) if vy<0 else 0\n                    return x+vx*dt*(1-np.clip(frac,0,1))\n                v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); d=bom*v\n                vx+=(-d*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt\n            return x\n        \n        def loss(p):\n            x0,y0,vx0,vy0,bom=p; dl=0; x,y=obs_x[-1],obs_y[-1]; vx,vy=vx0,vy0; t=obs_t[-1]; dt=0.002\n            for i in range(len(obs_t)-2,-1,-1):\n                while t>obs_t[i]:\n                    v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); d=bom*v\n                    vx-=(-d*vx)*dt; vy-=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x-=vx*dt; y-=vy*dt; t-=dt\n                dl+=(x-obs_x[i])**2+(y-obs_y[i])**2\n            return dl/max(len(obs_t),1)+0.1*((bom-200)/100)**2\n        \n        init=[obs_x[-1],obs_y[-1],vxi,vyi,bomi]\n        bnds=[(obs_x[-1]-2,obs_x[-1]+2),(obs_y[-1]-1,obs_y[-1]+1),(-50,80),(-60,20),(50,500)]\n        res=minimize(loss,init,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bnds,options={'maxiter':50})\n        return fwd(res.x if res.success else init)\n    except: return obs_x[-1]+2.0\n\ndef fusion_bayesian_averaging(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    n=len(preds); med=np.median(preds); lik=np.ones(n)\n    for i in range(n):\n        if preds[i]<0 or preds[i]>15: lik[i]=0.01\n        else: lik[i]=np.exp(-(preds[i]-med)**2/2.0)\n    post=lik; post/=post.sum()\n    return np.sum(preds*post)\n\ndef fusion_stacking_ensemble(preds, obs_t, obs_x, obs_y):\n    med=np.median(preds); mad=np.median(np.abs(preds-med))*1.4826\n    if mad<0.05: mad=0.05\n    mask=np.abs(preds-med)<3*mad\n    if np.sum(mask)>=2:\n        ip=preds[mask]; d=np.abs(ip-med); w=np.exp(-d**2/(2*mad**2))\n        return np.sum(ip*w)/np.sum(w)\n    return med\n\n# ===== TRAJECTORY GENERATION (fast version) =====\ndef simulate_traj(m, b_drag, x0, y0, vx0, vy0, dt=0.002, max_t=3.0):\n    xs,ys,ts=[x0],[y0],[0.0]; x,y=x0,y0; vx,vy=vx0,vy0; t=0.0\n    while y>=0 and t<max_t and len(xs)<3000:\n        v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); df=b_drag*v/m\n        vx+=(-df*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-df*vy)*dt; x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt; t+=dt\n        xs.append(x); ys.append(y); ts.append(t)\n        if y<0:\n            frac=-ys[-2]/(ys[-1]-ys[-2]+1e-12)\n            xs[-1]=xs[-2]+frac*(xs[-1]-xs[-2]); ys[-1]=0.0\n            ts[-1]=ts[-2]+frac*(ts[-1]-ts[-2]); break\n    return np.array(ts),np.array(xs),np.array(ys)\n\ndef gen_traj(shot_type=None):\n    if shot_type is None:\n        shot_type=np.random.choice(['smash','clear','drop','drive','net_drop','half_smash'],\n                                     p=[0.20,0.18,0.20,0.17,0.10,0.15])\n    m=np.random.normal(5.05,0.12)/1000.0; m=np.clip(m,4.70/1000,5.50/1000)\n    vt=np.random.normal(6.85,0.25); vt=np.clip(vt,6.3,7.5)\n    b=m*9.81/(vt**2)*np.random.normal(1.0,0.03)\n    x0=np.random.triangular(0.0,0.5,3.0); y0=np.clip(np.random.normal(2.8,0.8),2.0,3.5)\n    \n    st=shot_type\n    if st=='smash': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(78,10),55,95); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(-8,4),-20,-3)\n    elif st=='half_smash': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(55,8),40,70); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(-5,5),-15,0)\n    elif st=='clear': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(60,8),40,75); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(25,8),10,45)\n    elif st=='drop': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(30,7),15,45); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(5,8),-10,20)\n    elif st=='drive': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(50,10),30,70); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(0,6),-10,10)\n    elif st=='net_drop': sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(15,5),5,25); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(15,10),0,35)\n    else: sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(40,15),10,80); ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(5,12),-15,40)\n    \n    ar=np.radians(ang); vx0=sp*np.cos(ar); vy0=sp*np.sin(ar)\n    wvx=np.random.normal(0,0.3); wvy=np.random.normal(0,0.15)\n    ts,xs,ys=simulate_traj(m,b,x0,y0,vx0+wvx,vy0+wvy)\n    \n    li=np.where(ys<=0)[0]\n    if len(li)>0:\n        li0=li[0]\n        if li0>0:\n            f=-ys[li0-1]/(ys[li0]-ys[li0-1]+1e-12)\n            tlx=xs[li0-1]+f*(xs[li0]-xs[li0-1])\n        else: tlx=xs[0]\n    else: tlx=xs[-1]-ys[-1]*(xs[-1]-xs[-2])/(ys[-1]-ys[-2]+1e-12)\n    tlx=np.clip(tlx,0,15)\n    \n    tf=len(ts); osf=np.random.beta(2,5); osi=max(0,min(int(osf*tf*0.4),tf-10))\n    sl=np.random.random()<0.75\n    oef=np.random.uniform(0.85,0.99) if sl else np.random.uniform(0.4,0.85)\n    oei=min(int(oef*tf),tf-1); oei=max(oei,osi+5)\n    \n    fps=np.random.choice([120,130,150,200],p=[0.3,0.4,0.2,0.1]); fi=1.0/fps\n    oi=[]; tt=ts[osi]\n    while tt<=ts[oei] and len(oi)<200:\n        idx=np.searchsorted(ts,tt)\n        if idx<len(ts): oi.append(min(idx,len(ts)-1))\n        tt+=fi\n    oi=np.array(oi)\n    ns=np.random.lognormal(np.log(1.2),0.4)/1000.0\n    ot=ts[oi]; ox=xs[oi]+np.random.normal(0,ns,len(oi))\n    oy=ys[oi]+np.random.normal(0,ns,len(oi))\n    \n    return {'shot_type':st,'true_landing_x':tlx,'noise_std_mm':ns*1000,'fps':fps,\n            'sees_landing':sl,'obs_t':ot,'obs_x':ox,'obs_y':oy,'n_frames':len(oi)}\n\n# ===== MAIN VALIDATION =====\nprint(\"Generating 1200 variable trajectories...\")\nnp.random.seed(42)\nN=1200\ntrajs=[gen_traj() for _ in range(N)]\n\nshot_types=[t['shot_type'] for t in trajs]\nspeeds=[t.get('speed',0) for t in trajs]\nnoises=[t['noise_std_mm'] for t in trajs]\nn_frames=[t['n_frames'] for t in trajs]\nlandings=[t['true_landing_x'] for t in trajs]\n\nprint(f\"Shots: {dict(zip(*np.unique(shot_types,return_counts=True)))}\")\nprint(f\"Noise: {np.mean(noises):.2f}±{np.std(noises):.2f}mm\")\nprint(f\"Frames: {np.mean(n_frames):.0f}±{np.std(n_frames):.0f}\")\nprint(f\"Landing: {np.mean(landings):.2f}±{np.std(landings):.2f}m\")\n\nprint(\"\\nRunning 1200 trajectories × 14 methods...\")\nt0=time.time()\nME=np.zeros((N,6)); FE=np.zeros((N,8))\n\nfor idx,traj in enumerate(trajs):\n    ot,ox,oy=traj['obs_t'],traj['obs_x'],traj['obs_y']; tx=traj['true_landing_x']\n    preds=np.array([predict_polynomial(ot,ox,oy),predict_physical_fixed(ot,ox,oy),\n                    predict_physical_online(ot,ox,oy),predict_kalman_extrapolation(ot,ox,oy),\n                    predict_spline_extrapolation(ot,ox,oy),predict_velocity_damping(ot,ox,oy)])\n    ME[idx]=np.abs(preds-tx)\n    fp=np.zeros(8)\n    fp[0]=fusion_simple_average(preds)\n    fp[1]=fusion_cross_scoring(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[2]=fusion_weighted_average(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[3]=fusion_consensus_ransac(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[4]=fusion_iterative_refinement(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[5]=fusion_global_joint_optimization(ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[6]=fusion_bayesian_averaging(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    fp[7]=fusion_stacking_ensemble(preds,ot,ox,oy)\n    FE[idx]=np.abs(fp-tx)\n    if (idx+1)%300==0:\n        e=time.time()-t0\n        print(f\"  {idx+1}/{N} ({e:.1f}s)\")\n\ne=time.time()-t0\nprint(f\"\\nComplete! {N}×14 = {N*14} predictions in {e:.1f}s ({e/N*1000:.1f}ms/traj)\")\n\n# ===== STATISTICAL ANALYSIS =====\nMETHOD_NAMES=['M1_Poly','M2_PhysFixed','M3_PhysOnline','M4_Kalman','M5_Spline','M6_VelDamping']\nFUSION_NAMES=['F1_SimpleAvg','F2_CrossScoring','F3_WeightedAvg','F4_ConsensusRANSAC',\n              'F5_IterativeRefine','F6_JointOptimize','F7_BayesianAvg','F8_StackingEnsemble']\n\nprint(\"\\n\"+\"=\"*100)\nprint(\"FULL STATISTICAL RESULTS - ALL METHODS COMPARISON\")\nprint(\"=\"*100)\n\nall_errors=np.hstack([ME,FE])\nall_names=METHOD_NAMES+FUSION_NAMES\n\n# Sort by median error\nmedians=np.median(all_errors,axis=0)\norder=np.argsort(medians)\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'Rank':<5} {'Method':<25} {'Median(mm)':<12} {'Mean(mm)':<12} {'Std(mm)':<12} {'<40mm%':<10} {'<100mm%':<10} {'Max(m)':<10}\")\nprint(\"-\"*110)\nfor rank,idx in enumerate(order):\n    errs=all_errors[:,idx]\n    med=np.median(errs)*1000; mn=np.mean(errs)*1000; sd=np.std(errs)*1000\n    p40=np.mean(errs<0.04)*100; p100=np.mean(errs<0.1)*100; mx=np.max(errs)\n    star=\" ★\" if rank==0 else \"\"\n    print(f\"{rank+1:<5} {all_names[idx]:<25} {med:<12.1f} {mn:<12.1f} {sd:<12.1f} {p40:<10.1f} {p100:<10.1f} {mx:<10.2f}{star}\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\"+\"=\"*100)\nprint(\"INDIVIDUAL METHODS RANKING\")\nprint(\"=\"*100)\nm_order=np.argsort(np.median(ME,axis=0))\nfor rank,idx in enumerate(m_order):\n    errs=ME[:,idx]\n    print(f\"  {rank+1}. {METHOD_NAMES[idx]:<20} Median={np.median(errs)*1000:.1f}mm  Mean={np.mean(errs)*1000:.1f}mm  <40mm={np.mean(errs<0.04)*100:.1f}%\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\"+\"=\"*100)\nprint(\"FUSION METHODS RANKING\")\nprint(\"=\"*100)\nf_order=np.argsort(np.median(FE,axis=0))\nfor rank,idx in enumerate(f_order):\n    errs=FE[:,idx]\n    print(f\"  {rank+1}. {FUSION_NAMES[idx]:<25} Median={np.median(errs)*1000:.1f}mm  Mean={np.mean(errs)*1000:.1f}mm  <40mm={np.mean(errs<0.04)*100:.1f}%\")\n\n# Best individual vs best fusion\nbest_m_idx=m_order[0]; best_f_idx=f_order[0]\nprint(f\"\\nBest Individual: {METHOD_NAMES[best_m_idx]} (Median={np.median(ME[:,best_m_idx])*1000:.1f}mm)\")\nprint(f\"Best Fusion:     {FUSION_NAMES[best_f_idx]} (Median={np.median(FE[:,best_f_idx])*1000:.1f}mm)\")\nprint(f\"Improvement:     {((np.median(ME[:,best_m_idx])-np.median(FE[:,best_f_idx]))/np.median(ME[:,best_m_idx])*100):.1f}%\")\n\n# Breakdown by shot type\nprint(\"\\n\"+\"=\"*100)\nprint(\"PERFORMANCE BY SHOT TYPE\")\nprint(\"=\"*100)\nfor st in ['smash','clear','drop','drive','half_smash','net_drop']:\n    mask=np.array([t['shot_type']==st for t in trajs])\n    if np.sum(mask)>0:\n        bm=np.median(ME[mask],axis=0); m_i=np.argmin(bm)\n        bf=np.median(FE[mask],axis=0); f_i=np.argmin(bf)\n        print(f\"\\n  {st.upper()} (n={np.sum(mask)}):\")\n        print(f\"    Best Individual: {METHOD_NAMES[m_i]} ({bm[m_i]*1000:.1f}mm)\")\n        print(f\"    Best Fusion:     {FUSION_NAMES[f_i]} ({bf[f_i]*1000:.1f}mm)\")\n        print(f\"    Top 3 Fusion:    {FUSION_NAMES[np.argsort(bf)[0]]} ({bf[np.argsort(bf)[0]]*1000:.1f}mm), \"\n              f\"{FUSION_NAMES[np.argsort(bf)[1]]} ({bf[np.argsort(bf)[1]]*1000:.1f}mm), \"\n              f\"{FUSION_NAMES[np.argsort(bf)[2]]} ({bf[np.argsort(bf)[2]]*1000:.1f}mm)\")\n\n# Save\nnp.savez('/output/full_results.npz',ME=ME,FE=FE,method_names=METHOD_NAMES,fusion_names=FUSION_NAMES)\nprint(\"\\nResults saved to /output/full_results.npz\")\n"

> **思考过程**
> 
> Still timing out. The physical simulation with RK4 and the optimization-based methods are too slow. Let me:
> 1. Reduce to 500 trajectories
> 2. Pre-generate and save trajectories
> 3. Use even faster method implementations
> 4. Remove the most expensive fusion method (F6 JointOptimize is very slow due to scipy.optimize.minimize) or make it much faster
> 
> Let me try a much leaner approach.

    "code": "\nimport numpy as np, pickle, os, time\nos.makedirs('/output', exist_ok=True)\nnp.random.seed(42)\nG=9.81\n\ndef sim_traj(m,b,x0,y0,vx0,vy0,dt=0.004,max_t=3.0):\n    xs,ys,ts=[x0],[y0],[0.0]; x,y=x0,y0; vx,vy=vx0,vy0; t=0.0\n    while y>=0 and t<max_t and len(xs)<2000:\n        v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); d=b*v/m\n        vx+=(-d*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt; t+=dt\n        xs.append(x); ys.append(y); ts.append(t)\n        if y<0:\n            f=-ys[-2]/(ys[-1]-ys[-2]+1e-12)\n            xs[-1]=xs[-2]+f*(xs[-1]-xs[-2]); ys[-1]=0.0; ts[-1]=ts[-2]+f*(ts[-1]-ts[-2]); break\n    return np.array(ts),np.array(xs),np.array(ys)\n\ndef gen_traj():\n    st=np.random.choice(['smash','clear','drop','drive','net_drop','half_smash'],\n                         p=[0.20,0.18,0.20,0.17,0.10,0.15])\n    m=np.clip(np.random.normal(5.05,0.12)/1000,4.7/1000,5.5/1000)\n    vt=np.clip(np.random.normal(6.85,0.25),6.3,7.5); b=m*9.81/vt**2*np.random.normal(1,0.03)\n    x0=np.random.triangular(0,0.5,3); y0=np.clip(np.random.normal(2.8,0.8),2,3.5)\n    d={'smash':(78,10,-8,4,-20,-3),'half_smash':(55,8,-5,5,-15,0),\n       'clear':(60,8,25,8,10,45),'drop':(30,7,5,8,-10,20),\n       'drive':(50,10,0,6,-10,10),'net_drop':(15,5,15,10,0,35)}\n    mu_s,si_s,mu_a,si_a,al,ah=d.get(st,(40,15,5,12,-15,40))\n    sp=np.clip(np.random.normal(mu_s,si_s),5,100)\n    ang=np.clip(np.random.normal(mu_a,si_a),al,ah)\n    ar=np.radians(ang); vx0=sp*np.cos(ar)+np.random.normal(0,0.3)\n    vy0=sp*np.sin(ar)+np.random.normal(0,0.15)\n    ts,xs,ys=sim_traj(m,b,x0,y0,vx0,vy0)\n    li=np.where(ys<=0)[0]\n    if len(li)>0:\n        li0=li[0]\n        tlx=xs[li0-1]+(-ys[li0-1]/(ys[li0]-ys[li0-1]+1e-12))*(xs[li0]-xs[li0-1]) if li0>0 else xs[0]\n    else: tlx=xs[-1]-ys[-1]*(xs[-1]-xs[-2])/(ys[-1]-ys[-2]+1e-12)\n    tlx=np.clip(tlx,0,15)\n    tf=len(ts); osf=np.random.beta(2,5); osi=max(0,min(int(osf*tf*0.4),tf-10))\n    sl=np.random.random()<0.75; oef=np.random.uniform(0.85,0.99) if sl else np.random.uniform(0.4,0.85)\n    oei=min(int(oef*tf),tf-1); oei=max(oei,osi+5)\n    fps=np.random.choice([120,130,150,200],p=[0.3,0.4,0.2,0.1]); fi=1.0/fps\n    oi=[]; tt=ts[osi]\n    while tt<=ts[oei] and len(oi)<200:\n        idx=np.searchsorted(ts,tt)\n        if idx<len(ts): oi.append(min(idx,len(ts)-1))\n        tt+=fi\n    oi=np.array(oi)\n    ns=np.random.lognormal(np.log(1.2),0.4)/1000\n    ot=ts[oi]; ox=xs[oi]+np.random.normal(0,ns,len(oi)); oy=ys[oi]+np.random.normal(0,ns,len(oi))\n    return {'shot_type':st,'true_landing_x':tlx,'noise_std_mm':ns*1000,\n            'obs_t':ot,'obs_x':ox,'obs_y':oy,'n_frames':len(oi)}\n\nprint(\"Generating 800 trajectories...\")\nt0=time.time()\ntrajs=[gen_traj() for _ in range(800)]\nprint(f\"Done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\nwith open('/output/trajs_800.pkl','wb') as f: pickle.dump(trajs,f)\nprint(\"Saved.\")\nshots=[t['shot_type'] for t in trajs]; nf=[t['n_frames'] for t in trajs]\nnoises=[t['noise_std_mm'] for t in trajs]; lx=[t['true_landing_x'] for t in trajs]\nprint(f\"Shots: {dict(zip(*np.unique(shots,return_counts=True)))}\")\nprint(f\"Frames: {np.mean(nf):.0f}±{np.std(nf):.0f} | Noise: {np.mean(noises):.2f}±{np.std(noises):.2f}mm | Landing: {np.mean(lx):.2f}±{np.std(lx):.2f}m\")\nprint(\"DATA SUMMARY: Everything varies. Nothing is fixed. This mirrors real badminton.\")\n",

d=bom*v\n            vx+=(-d*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt\n        return x\n    except: return ox[-1]+2\n\ndef m4_kalman(ot,ox,oy):\n    try:\n        n=len(ot)\n        if n<3: return ox[-1]+2\n        s=max(1.0,n/30.0); xs=gaussian_filter1d(ox,sigma=s); ys=gaussian_filter1d(oy,sigma=s)\n        vx=(xs[-1]-xs[-4])/(ot[-1]-ot[-4]) if n>=4 else (xs[-1]-xs[0])/(ot[-1]-ot[0])\n        vy=(ys[-1]-ys[-4])/(ot[-1]-ot[-4]) if n>=4 else (ys[-1]-ys[0])/(ot[-1]-ot[0])\n        if vy>=0: vy=-2.0\n        return ox[-1]+vx*(-oy[-1]/vy)\n    except: return ox[-1]+2\n\ndef m5_spline(ot,ox,oy):\n    try:\n        n=len(ot); k=min(3,n-1)\n        if n<4: return ox[-1]+2\n        sp_x=UnivariateSpline(ot,ox,k=k,s=0.5); sp_y=UnivariateSpline(ot,oy,k=k,s=0.5)\n        dt=0.01; t=ot[-1]; x,y=ox[-1],oy[-1]\n        for _ in range(600):\n            if y<=0: vy=sp_y.derivative()(min(t,ot[-1]+0.5)); return x if vy>=0 else x-y*sp_x.derivative()(min(t,ot[-1]+0.5))/vy\n            t+=dt; d=np.exp(-0.3*(t-ot[-1]))\n            x+=sp_x.derivative()(min(t,ot[-1]+0.5))*dt*d\n            y+=sp_y.derivative()(min(t,ot[-1]+0.5))*dt*d\n            if y>100 or x>30: break\n        return x\n    except: return ox[-1]+2\n\ndef m6_veldamp(ot,ox,oy):\n    try:\n        n=len(ot)\n        if n<3: return ox[-1]+2\n        dts=np.diff(ot); vxs=np.diff(ox)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n        vys=np.diff(oy)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None); sps=np.sqrt(vxs**2+vys**2); tm=ot[:-1]+dts/2\n        if len(sps)>3:\n            ls=np.log(np.clip(sps-6.5,0.5,None)); vld=np.isfinite(ls)\n            k=-np.polyfit(tm[vld],ls[vld],1)[0] if np.sum(vld)>2 else 1.5\n        else: k=1.5\n        k=np.clip(k,0.2,10)\n        vx=vxs[-1]; vy=vys[-1]; x=ox[-1]; y=oy[-1]; t=ot[-1]; dt=0.004\n        for _ in range(1250):\n            if y<=0: f=-y/(vy*dt+1e-12) if vy<0 else 0; return x+vx*dt*(1-np.clip(f,0,1))\n            sp=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2)\n            if sp>1e-6:\n                vxd=vx/sp; vyd=vy/sp; tgt=sps[0]*np.exp(-k*(t-ot[0]))+6.5\n                ns=0.95*sp+0.05*tgt; vx=vxd*ns; vy=vyd*ns\n            x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt; t+=dt; vy-=G*dt*0.3\n        return x\n    except: return ox[-1]+2\n\n# ===== FUSION METHODS =====\ndef f1_simple_avg(p): return np.mean(p)\n\ndef f2_cross_scoring(p,ot,ox,oy):\n    n=len(p); s=np.zeros(n)\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(n):\n            if i==j: continue\n            d=abs(p[j]-p[i]); tol=0.3+3.0/np.sqrt(max(len(ot),1))\n            s[j]+=np.exp(-d**2/(2*tol**2))*10\n    s+=2; return p[np.argmax(s)]\n\ndef f3_weighted_avg(p,ot,ox,oy):\n    med=np.median(p); mad=np.median(np.abs(p-med))*1.4826\n    if mad<0.01: mad=0.01\n    w=np.exp(-(p-med)**2/(2*mad**2)); w/=w.sum()\n    return np.sum(p*w)\n\ndef f4_ransac(p,ot,ox,oy,n_iter=50):\n    n=len(p)\n    if n<3: return np.median(p)\n    bc=0; bx=np.median(p)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        idx=np.random.choice(n,min(3,n),replace=False); c=np.mean(p[idx])\n        inl=np.sum(np.abs(p-c)<0.5)\n        if inl>bc: bc=inl; bx=c\n    mask=np.abs(p-bx)<0.5\n    return np.mean(p[mask]) if np.sum(mask)>=2 else np.median(p)\n\ndef f5_iter_refine(p,ot,ox,oy,n_iter=5):\n    r=p.copy().astype(float)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        nr=r.copy()\n        for i in range(len(p)):\n            pull=0; tw=0\n            for j in range(len(p)):\n                if i==j: continue\n                w=np.exp(-(r[i]-r[j])**2/0.5); pull+=r[j]*w; tw+=w\n            if tw>0: nr[i]=0.7*r[i]+0.3*pull/tw\n        r=nr\n    return np.mean(r)\n\ndef f6_joint_opt(ot,ox,oy):\n    try:\n        n=len(ot)\n        if n<3: return ox[-1]+2\n        dt_l=ot[-1]-ot[-2] if n>1 else 0.01\n        vxi=(ox[-1]-ox[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else 20; vyi=(oy[-1]-oy[-2])/dt_l if n>1 else -5\n        if n>3:\n            dts=np.diff(ot); vxs=np.diff(ox)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None)\n            axs=np.gradient(vxs,dts); vs=np.sqrt(vxs**2+(np.diff(oy)/np.clip(dts,1e-6,None))**2)\n            bomi=np.median(-axs/np.clip(vs*vxs,1e-3,None))\n            bomi=np.clip(bomi,50,500) if np.isfinite(bomi) else 200\n        else: bomi=200\n        \n        def fwd(p):\n            x,y,vx,vy,bom=p; dt=0.004\n            for _ in range(1250):\n                if y<=0: f=-y/(vy*dt+1e-12) if vy<0 else 0; return x+vx*dt*(1-np.clip(f,0,1))\n                v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); d=bom*v\n                vx+=(-d*vx)*dt; vy+=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x+=vx*dt; y+=vy*dt\n            return x\n        \n        def loss(p):\n            _,_,vx0,vy0,bom=p; dl=0; x,y=ox[-1],oy[-1]; vx,vy=vx0,vy0; t=ot[-1]; dt=0.004\n            for i in range(len(ot)-2,-1,-1):\n                while t>ot[i]:\n                    v=np.sqrt(vx**2+vy**2); d=bom*v\n                    vx-=(-d*vx)*dt; vy-=(-G-d*vy)*dt; x-=vx*dt; y-=vy*dt; t-=dt\n                dl+=(x-ox[i])**2+(y-oy[i])**2\n            return dl/max(len(ot),1)+0.1*((bom-200)/100)**2\n        \n        init=[ox[-1],oy[-1],vxi,vyi,bomi]\n        bnds=[(ox[-1]-2,ox[-1]+2),(oy[-1]-1,oy[-1]+1),(-50,80),(-60,20),(50,500)]\n        res=minimize(loss,init,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bnds,options={'maxiter':30})\n        return fwd(res.x if res.success else init)\n    except: return ox[-1]+2\n\ndef f7_bayes(p,ot,ox,oy):\n    med=np.median(p); lik=np.ones(len(p))\n    for i in range(len(p)):\n        if p[i]<0 or p[i]>15: lik[i]=0.01\n        else: lik[i]=np.exp(-(p[i]-med)**2/2.0)\n    post=lik; post/=post.sum()\n    return np.sum(p*post)\n\ndef f8_stacking(p,ot,ox,oy):\n    med=np.median(p); mad=np.median(np.abs(p-med))*1.4826\n    if mad<0.05: mad=0.05\n    mask=np.abs(p-med)<3*mad\n    if np.sum(mask)>=2:\n        ip=p[mask]; d=np.abs(ip-med); w=np.exp(-d**2/(2*mad**2))\n        return np.sum(ip*w)/np.sum(w)\n    return med\n\n# ===== LOAD & RUN =====\nprint(\"Loading 800 trajectories...\")\nwith open('/input/trajs_800.pkl','rb') as f: trajs=pickle.load(f)\nN=len(trajs)\nprint(f\"Loaded {N} trajectories. Running validation...\")\n\nMETHODS=[m1_poly,m2_phys_fixed,m3_phys_online,m4_kalman,m5_spline,m6_veldamp]\nFUSIONS=[f1_simple_avg,f2_cross_scoring,f3_weighted_avg,f4_ransac,\n         f5_iter_refine,f6_joint_opt,f7_bayes,f8_stacking]\nMNAMES=['M1_Poly','M2_PhysFixed','M3_PhysOnline','M4_Kalman','M5_Spline','M6_VelDamp']\nFNAMES=['F1_SimpleAvg','F2_CrossScoring','F3_WeightedAvg','F4_RANSAC',\n        'F5_IterRefine','F6_JointOptimize','F7_Bayesian','F8_Stacking']\n\nME=np.zeros((N,6)); FE=np.zeros((N,8))\nt0=time.time()\n\nfor idx,t in enumerate(trajs):\n    ot,ox,oy,tx=t['obs_t'],t['obs_x'],t['obs_y'],t['true_landing_x']\n    preds=np.array([m(ot,ox,oy) for m in METHODS])\n    ME[idx]=np.abs(preds-tx)\n    fp=np.array([f(preds,ot,ox,oy) for f in FUSIONS])\n    FE[idx]=np.abs(fp-tx)\n    if (idx+1)%200==0: print(f\"  {idx+1}/{N} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)\")\n\net=time.time()-t0\nprint(f\"\\nDone! {N} trajectories × 14 methods in {et:.1f}s ({et/N*1000:.1f}ms/traj)\")\n\n# ===== FULL STATISTICAL ANALYSIS =====\nprint(\"\\n\"+\"=\"*95)\nprint(\"COMPLETE STATISTICAL VALIDATION: 800 TRAJECTORIES × 14 METHODS\")\nprint(\"=\"*95)\n\nall_err=np.hstack([ME,FE]); all_names=MNAMES+FNAMES\nmedians=np.median(all_err,axis=0); order=np.argsort(medians)\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'Rank':<5} {'Method':<22} {'Median(mm)':<12} {'Mean(mm)':<12} {'Std(mm)':<12} {'<40mm%':<10} {'<100mm%':<10} {'<200mm%':<10} {'Max(m)':<8}\")\nprint(\"-\"*105)\nfor rank,i in enumerate(order):\n    e=all_err[:,i]; med=np.median(e)*1000; mn=np.mean(e)*1000; sd=np.std(e)*1000\n    p40=np.mean(e<0.04)*100; p100=np.mean(e<0.1)*100; p200=np.mean(e<0.2)*100; mx=np.max(e)\n    star=\" ★BEST\" if rank==0 else \"\"\n    print(f\"{rank+1:<5} {all_names[i]:<22} {med:<12.1f} {mn:<12.1f} {sd:<12.1f} {p40:<10.1f} {p100:<10.1f} {p200:<10.1f} {mx:<8.2f}{star}\")\n\n# Individual ranking\nprint(\"\\n--- INDIVIDUAL METHODS ---\")\nmo=np.argsort(np.median(ME,axis=0))\nfor r,i in enumerate(mo):\n    e=ME[:,i]; print(f\"  {r+1}. {MNAMES[i]:<18} Med={np.median(e)*1000:.1f}mm Mean={np.mean(e)*1000:.1f}mm <40mm={np.mean(e<0.04)*100:.1f}%\")\n\n# Fusion ranking\nprint(\"\\n--- FUSION METHODS ---\")\nfo=np.argsort(np.median(FE,axis=0))\nfor r,i in enumerate(fo):\n    e=FE[:,i]; print(f\"  {r+1}. {FNAMES[i]:<22} Med={np.median(e)*1000:.1f}mm Mean={np.mean(e)*1000:.1f}mm <40mm={np.mean(e<0.04)*100:.1f}%\")\n\n# Best comparison\nbmi=mo[0]; bfi=fo[0]\nbm_med=np.median(ME[:,bmi]); bf_med=np.median(FE[:,bfi])\nprint(f\"\\n--- KEY FINDING ---\")\nprint(f\"Best Individual:  {MNAMES[bmi]} (Median={bm_med*1000:.1f}mm)\")\nprint(f\"Best Fusion:      {FNAMES[bfi]} (Median={bf_med*1000:.1f}mm)\")\nimprovement=(bm_med-bf_med)/bm_med*100\nprint(f\"Fusion Advantage: {improvement:.1f}% improvement over best individual method\")\n\n# By shot type\nprint(\"\\n--- BY SHOT TYPE ---\")\nfor st in ['smash','clear','drop','drive','half_smash','net_drop']:\n    mask=np.array([t['shot_type']==st for t in trajs])\n    if np.sum(mask)>0:\n        bm=np.median(ME[mask],axis=0);
